GUNAROK SPRITE PROMPTS, ALL CHAMPIONS
Based on Gunarok Combat and Content Design Plan v2 (Section 10). 14 champions: 8 warriors, 6 gods.

HOW TO USE
1. For each champion, generate the REFERENCE prompt first and get the team to approve it.
2. Attach that approved reference image to EVERY animation prompt for that champion.
3. Generate one animation per image. Save it as <animation>.png (for example light.png).
   Layouts: 2 and 3 frames are one row; 4 frames are a 2x2 grid; 5 frames are 3 on top and 2 below;
   6 frames are a 3x2 grid. The commands already carry the matching --grid option.
4. Run the matching command from that champion's COMMANDS block, then check preview/<animation>.gif.
5. When a champion is done, run the build command at the end of the block.

SETTINGS (v2 spec, already the defaults in gunarok_sprites.py)
- Body about 84 px tall, canvas 192 x 128, 4 px padding, feet at bottom centre.
- Shown at 2x in Godot. Set Project Settings > Rendering > Textures > Default Texture Filter to Nearest.
- Up to 16 colors plus a dark outline, light from the top left, always drawn facing right.

RULES THAT APPLY TO EVERY CHAMPION
- Projectiles, slash trails, lightning, shockwaves and the legionaries are separate FX sprites.
  The prompts say not to draw them. If the generator draws them anyway, regenerate or erase them.
- Seize and throw rows must show only the champion. If an opponent appears, regenerate.
- The ultimate is 10 frames, generated as two rows of 5 (ultimate_1 and ultimate_2).
  The second command uses --append so both rows become one 'ultimate' animation.
- jump_light (Open Decision 8) and intro are optional. Leave them until the team decides.
- Rows with no standing frame (jump, skill_air, jump_light, knockdown, getup, ko) can come out
  the wrong size. Compare the preview with idle and re-run with --height <number> to fix.
- Simo and Zeus use a dark green background, because their white clothing is too close to light
  gray and would be erased with the background.
- If the generator ignores the grid and makes a single row anyway, drop --grid from the command.
- Crazy Horse and Bathala are draft looks for team review. Kabunian must not be generated until
  the team has done a respectful review of the design.

CONTENTS
   1. Leonidas (Warrior), id: leonidas
   2. Julius Caesar (Warrior), id: caesar
   3. Ragnar (Warrior), id: ragnar
   4. Miyamoto Musashi (Warrior), id: musashi
   5. Lapu-Lapu (Warrior), id: lapulapu
   6. Crazy Horse (Warrior), id: crazyhorse
   7. Simo Hayha (Warrior), id: simo
   8. Nai Khanom Tom (Warrior), id: naikhanomtom
   9. Zeus (God (final boss)), id: zeus
  10. Thor (God), id: thor
  11. Susanoo (God), id: susanoo
  12. Anubis (God), id: anubis
  13. Bathala (God), id: bathala
  14. Kabunian (God (hidden)), id: kabunian


====================================================================================================
1. LEONIDAS   [Warrior, Greece]   id: leonidas
====================================================================================================

NOTE: Reference already approved (the fighting-stance image with the level spear). Skip the REFERENCE prompt and attach that image.

--- REFERENCE (generate first, do not attach anything) ---
16-bit pixel art character sprite, SNES fighting game style: Leonidas, Spartan king: bronze Corinthian helmet with red crest, red cloak, bare muscular torso, dark leather skirt, sandals with leg wraps, bronze round shield with lambda emblem on his left arm, wooden spear with bronze tip in his right hand. Fighting stance, side view, facing right: shield held forward on the right covering the torso, spear held level at shoulder height pointing right over the shield, knees slightly bent, feet apart. Full body, single character, centered. Limited palette of about 16 colors with a dark outline, light from the top left, clean pixel outlines, no anti-aliasing. Plain flat light gray background, no shadow, no ground line, no text, no frame borders.

--- CUT-IN PORTRAIT (ultimate cut-in, 700 x 500, attach the reference) ---
16-bit pixel art portrait for a fighting game ultimate cut-in, wide 7:5 image: Leonidas, Spartan king: bronze Corinthian helmet with red crest, red cloak, bare muscular torso, dark leather skirt, sandals with leg wraps, bronze round shield with lambda emblem on his left arm, wooden spear with bronze tip in his right hand. Upper body from the waist up, dynamic pose: spear thrust forward past the shield, shouting, cloak flaring. Facing right, intense expression, head near the top edge. Limited palette with a dark outline, light from the top left, clean pixel outlines. Plain flat light gray background, no text. (Remove the background afterwards to make a transparent PNG.)

--- idle (4 frames) ---
16-bit pixel art, SNES fighting game style. Sprite sheet: a 2 by 2 grid of exactly 4 frames, 2 frames in the top row and 2 in the bottom row, read left to right then top to bottom. Every frame the same size with wide empty space between frames and between the two rows, so no figures touch or overlap. Same character at the same scale in every frame, matching the attached reference: Leonidas, Spartan king: bronze Corinthian helmet with red crest, red cloak, bare muscular torso, dark leather skirt, sandals with leg wraps, bronze round shield with lambda emblem on his left arm, wooden spear with bronze tip in his right hand. Side view, facing right, full body in every frame, feet at the same ground level. Animation: breathing idle in the fighting stance (shield held forward on the right covering the torso, spear held level at shoulder height pointing right over the shield, knees slightly bent, feet apart), slight up-and-down bob, clothing and hair moving gently. Limited palette of about 16 colors with a dark outline, light from the top left, clean pixel outlines, no anti-aliasing. Plain flat light gray background, no shadow, no ground line, no text, no frame borders.

python gunarok_sprites.py process crouch_block.png --champion zeus --anim crouch_block --frames 2
python gunarok_sprites.py process hitstun.png --champion zeus --anim hitstun --frames 2
python gunarok_sprites.py process knockdown.png --champion zeus --anim knockdown --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process getup.png --champion zeus --anim getup --frames 3
python gunarok_sprites.py process ko.png --champion zeus --anim ko --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process victory.png --champion zeus --anim victory --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process parry.png --champion zeus --anim parry --frames 3
python gunarok_sprites.py process parry_fail.png --champion zeus --anim parry_fail --frames 3
python gunarok_sprites.py process parried.png --champion zeus --anim parried --frames 3
python gunarok_sprites.py process grabbed.png --champion zeus --anim grabbed --frames 2
python gunarok_sprites.py process light.png --champion zeus --anim light --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process heavy.png --champion zeus --anim heavy --frames 6 --grid 3x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process seize.png --champion zeus --anim seize --frames 5 --grid 3x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process seize_throw.png --champion zeus --anim seize_throw --frames 6 --grid 3x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process crouch_light.png --champion zeus --anim crouch_light --frames 3
python gunarok_sprites.py process crouch_heavy.png --champion zeus --anim crouch_heavy --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process skill_ground.png --champion zeus --anim skill_ground --frames 6 --grid 3x2

python gunarok_sprites.py build --champion zeus

10. THOR   [God, Scandinavia (shares the Fjord map)]   id: thor
====================================================================================================

--- REFERENCE (generate first, do not attach anything) ---
16-bit pixel art character sprite, SNES fighting game style: Thor, Norse god of thunder as in the old myths, not the modern comic look: huge muscular build, wild long red hair and braided red beard, iron chainmail shirt under a brown leather jerkin, thick iron belt, dark gray trousers with leg wraps, iron gauntlets, a short-handled iron war hammer in his right hand. Fighting stance, side view, facing right: hammer raised beside the head, left fist forward, wide planted stance. Full body, single character, centered. Limited palette of about 16 colors with a dark outline, light from the top left, clean pixel outlines, no anti-aliasing. Plain flat light gray background, no shadow, no ground line, no text, no frame borders.

--- CUT-IN PORTRAIT (ultimate cut-in, 700 x 500, attach the reference) ---

Upper body from the waist up, dynamic pose: hammer raised high, roaring, beard and hair flying. Facing right, intense expression, head near the top edge. Side view, facing right, full body in every frame, feet at the same ground level. python gunarok_sprites.py process block.png --champion thor --anim block --frames 2

python gunarok_sprites.py process hitstun.png --champion thor --anim hitstun --frames 2

python gunarok_sprites.py process ko.png --champion thor --anim ko --frames 4 --grid 2x2
python gunarok_sprites.py process victory.png --champion thor --anim victory --frames 4 --grid 2x2
